13) CLASSIFICATION POLICY FOR NEXT PHASE
------------------------------------------------------------
AUTHORITATIVE_POLICY:
  1. Latest implemented NDSP contracts are candidates for canonical authority.
  2. Historical regression baseline is evidence, not automatic target state.
  3. A missing historical field is NOT automatically restored.
  4. Existing modern capability not exposed by 9082 is a wiring candidate.
  5. Duplicate contract implementations must be reconciled by version,
     implementation recency, runtime use, and governance role.
  6. 9082 becomes the canonical composed contract.
  7. Public API must preserve parity with canonical 9082.
  8. Only after canonical validation do we create a NEW explicit baseline.
  9. Previous baseline remains immutable as historical evidence.
 10. No runtime mutation was performed by this discovery script.
